## Psalms 1:1 (id: 10019001001)

## Psalms 1:5 (id: 10019001005)

## Psalms 1:6 (id: 10019001006)

## Psalms 28:1 (id: 10019028001)

## Psalms 31:16 (id: 10019031016)

## Psalms 31:17 (id: 10019031017)

## Psalms 31:18 (id: 10019031018)

## Psalms 38:0 (id: 10019038000)

0A psalm of David; to bring to remembrance.


## Psalms 38:1 (id: 10019038001)

1Yahweh, do not rebuke me in your anger, nor discipline me in your wrath.


## Psalms 38:2 (id: 10019038002)

2For your arrows have sunk into me, and your hand has come down on me.


## Psalms 38:3 (id: 10019038003)

## Psalms 38:4 (id: 10019038004)

## Psalms 38:5 (id: 10019038005)

## Psalms 38:6 (id: 10019038006)

## Psalms 38:8 (id: 10019038008)

## Psalms 38:9 (id: 10019038009)

## Psalms 38:12 (id: 10019038012)

## Psalms 38:13 (id: 10019038013)

## Psalms 38:14 (id: 10019038014)

## Psalms 38:15 (id: 10019038015)

## Psalms 38:16 (id: 10019038016)

## Psalms 38:17 (id: 10019038017)

## Psalms 38:18 (id: 10019038018)

## Psalms 38:19 (id: 10019038019)

## Psalms 38:20 (id: 10019038020)

## Psalms 38:21 (id: 10019038021)

## Psalms 38:22 (id: 10019038022)

## Psalms 105:1 (id: 10019105001)

## Psalms 105:4 (id: 10019105004)

## Psalms 105:6 (id: 10019105006)

## Psalms 105:7 (id: 10019105007)

## Psalms 105:8 (id: 10019105008)

## Psalms 105:9 (id: 10019105009)

9that he cut with Abraham and his oath to Isaac.


## Psalms 105:10 (id: 10019105010)

## Psalms 105:11 (id: 10019105011)

## Psalms 105:12 (id: 10019105012)

12when they were few in number, very few, and strangers in it.


## Psalms 105:13 (id: 10019105013)

## Psalms 105:14 (id: 10019105014)

## Psalms 105:15 (id: 10019105015)

## Psalms 105:16 (id: 10019105016)

## Psalms 105:20 (id: 10019105020)

## Psalms 105:21 (id: 10019105021)

## Psalms 105:24 (id: 10019105024)

## Psalms 105:29 (id: 10019105029)

## Psalms 105:30 (id: 10019105030)

## Psalms 105:31 (id: 10019105031)

## Psalms 105:32 (id: 10019105032)

## Psalms 105:33 (id: 10019105033)

## Psalms 105:34 (id: 10019105034)

## Psalms 105:35 (id: 10019105035)

## Psalms 105:36 (id: 10019105036)

## Psalms 105:37 (id: 10019105037)

## Psalms 105:39 (id: 10019105039)

## Psalms 105:40 (id: 10019105040)

## Psalms 106:1 (id: 10019106001)

1Hallelu Yah! Give thanks to Yahweh, for {he is} good, for his covenant faithfulness {is} to forever.


## Psalms 106:2 (id: 10019106002)

## Psalms 106:3 (id: 10019106003)

3The happinesses of the guardians of justice, the doers of righteousness at all times!


## Psalms 106:4 (id: 10019106004)

4Remember me, Yahweh, in the favor of your people; visit me in your salvation,


## Psalms 106:5 (id: 10019106005)

5to look on the good of your chosen, to rejoice in the gladness of your nation, to boast with your inheritance.


## Psalms 106:6 (id: 10019106006)

## Psalms 106:7 (id: 10019106007)

7Our fathers did not consider your marvelous deeds in Egypt; they did not remember the abundance of your covenant faithfulness, and they rebelled at the sea, the Sea of Reeds.


## Psalms 106:8 (id: 10019106008)

8But he saved them on account of his name, to make known his power.


## Psalms 106:9 (id: 10019106009)

## Psalms 106:11 (id: 10019106011)

## Psalms 106:12 (id: 10019106012)

12Then they believed his words; they sang his praise.


## Psalms 106:13 (id: 10019106013)

13But they hurried, they forgot his deeds; they did not wait for his instructions.


## Psalms 106:14 (id: 10019106014)

14And they craved a craving in the wilderness, and they tested God in the wasteland.


## Psalms 106:15 (id: 10019106015)

15So he gave to them their request, but he sent a wasting disease upon their lives.


## Psalms 106:16 (id: 10019106016)

16And they became envious of Moses in the camp, of Aaron, the holy one of Yahweh.


## Psalms 106:17 (id: 10019106017)

17The earth opened and swallowed Dathan and covered over the congregation of Abiram.


## Psalms 106:18 (id: 10019106018)

18Fire blazed up in their congregation; a flame burned up the wicked.


## Psalms 106:19 (id: 10019106019)

19They made a calf at Horeb and worshiped a cast image.


## Psalms 106:20 (id: 10019106020)

20Thus they traded their Glory for the likeness of a bull eating grass.


## Psalms 106:21 (id: 10019106021)

21They forgot God their Savior, the doer of great {deeds} in Egypt:


## Psalms 106:22 (id: 10019106022)

22wonders in the land of Ham, awesome {acts} at the Sea of Reeds.


## Psalms 106:23 (id: 10019106023)

23So he said to annihilate them— had not Moses, his chosen one, stood in the breach to his face, to turn away his heat from destroying.


## Psalms 106:24 (id: 10019106024)

24Then they despised the delightful land; they did not believe his word,


## Psalms 106:25 (id: 10019106025)

25but grumbled in their tents; they did not listen to the voice of Yahweh.


## Psalms 106:26 (id: 10019106026)

26Then he raised his hand to them to make them fall in the desert,


## Psalms 106:27 (id: 10019106027)

27and to make their seed fall among the nations, and to scatter them among the lands.


## Psalms 106:28 (id: 10019106028)

28They joined themselves to the Baal of Peor and ate the sacrifices of the dead.


## Psalms 106:29 (id: 10019106029)

29And they angered with their actions, and a plague broke out among them.


## Psalms 106:30 (id: 10019106030)

30Then Phinehas stood and intervened, and the plague was restrained.


## Psalms 106:31 (id: 10019106031)

31It was counted to him as righteousness to generation and generation until forever.


## Psalms 106:32 (id: 10019106032)

32And they angered at the waters of Meribah, and it was bad for Moses on account of them.


## Psalms 106:33 (id: 10019106033)

33For they rebelled against his spirit, and he spoke rashly with his lips.


## Psalms 106:34 (id: 10019106034)

34They did not destroy the peoples, which Yahweh had said to them,


## Psalms 106:35 (id: 10019106035)

35but they mingled with the nations and learned their ways


## Psalms 106:36 (id: 10019106036)

36and served their idols, and they became a snare to them.


## Psalms 106:37 (id: 10019106037)

37And they sacrificed their sons and their daughters to demons.


## Psalms 106:38 (id: 10019106038)

38And they shed innocent blood, the blood of their sons and their daughters, whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan, and the land was desecrated by bloodshed.


## Psalms 106:39 (id: 10019106039)

39And they became unclean by their deeds; and they prostituted themselves by their actions.


## Psalms 106:40 (id: 10019106040)

40So Yahweh’s nose burned with his people, and he despised his inheritance.


## Psalms 106:41 (id: 10019106041)

41And he gave them into the hand of the nations, and the haters of them ruled over them.


## Psalms 106:42 (id: 10019106042)

42And their enemies oppressed them, and they were humbled under their hand.


## Psalms 106:43 (id: 10019106043)

43Many times he rescued them, but they, they were rebellious in their counsel and sank in their sin.


## Psalms 106:44 (id: 10019106044)

44Yet he saw in the narrow to them, when he was hearing their cry.


## Psalms 106:45 (id: 10019106045)

45And he remembered his covenant with them and relented because of the abundance of his covenant faithfulness.


## Psalms 106:46 (id: 10019106046)

46He gave them for compassion to the face of all of their captors.


## Psalms 106:47 (id: 10019106047)

47Save us, Yahweh, our God! Gather us from the nations to give thanks to the name of your holiness, to glory in your praises.


## Psalms 106:48 (id: 10019106048)

48May Yahweh, the God of Israel, be blessed from everlasting and to everlasting, and all the people say, “Amen. Hallelu Yah!”

10Sitting in darkness and gloom, prisoners of affliction and iron

## Psalms 107:14 (id: 10019107014)

15May they thank Yahweh {for} his covenant faithfulness and his amazing deeds for the sons of man!

16For he has broken the gates of bronze and cut through the bars of iron.


## Psalms 107:17 (id: 10019107017)

17Foolish from their rebellious way and afflicted from their sins,

21May they thank Yahweh {for} his covenant faithfulness and his amazing deeds for the sons of man!

## Psalms 107:23 (id: 10019107023)

23Going down to the sea in ships, doing business on abundant waters,

26They went up {to} the sky; they went down {to} the depths. Their souls melted in the disaster.

## Psalms 107:28 (id: 10019107028)

## Psalms 107:29 (id: 10019107029)

29He raised the storm to calmness and he quieted the waves.


## Psalms 107:30 (id: 10019107030)

## Psalms 107:31 (id: 10019107031)

31May they thank Yahweh {for} his covenant faithfulness and his amazing deeds for the sons of man!


## Psalms 107:32 (id: 10019107032)

32May they exalt him in the assembly of the people and praise him at the seat of the elders.


## Psalms 107:33 (id: 10019107033)

33He turns rivers into a desert and springs of water into thirsty land,


## Psalms 107:34 (id: 10019107034)

34fruitful ground into salt land, from the wickedness of the dwellers in it.


## Psalms 107:35 (id: 10019107035)

35He turns the wilderness into a pool of water and dry land into springs of water.


## Psalms 107:36 (id: 10019107036)

## Psalms 107:37 (id: 10019107037)

## Psalms 107:42 (id: 10019107042)

## Psalms 108:0 (id: 10019108000)

0A song; a psalm of David.


## Psalms 108:1 (id: 10019108001)

## Psalms 108:2 (id: 10019108002)

2Awaken, lyre and harp; I will awaken the dawn.


## Psalms 108:3 (id: 10019108003)

## Psalms 108:4 (id: 10019108004)

4For your covenant faithfulness {is} great from above the heavens, and your trustworthiness, above the clouds.


## Psalms 108:5 (id: 10019108005)

## Psalms 108:6 (id: 10019108006)

## Psalms 108:7 (id: 10019108007)

## Psalms 108:8 (id: 10019108008)

## Psalms 108:9 (id: 10019108009)

## Psalms 108:10 (id: 10019108010)

## Psalms 108:11 (id: 10019108011)

## Psalms 108:12 (id: 10019108012)

## Psalms 108:13 (id: 10019108013)

## Psalms 109:1 (id: 10019109001)

1To the chief musician: a psalm of David. God of my praise, do not be silent!


## Psalms 109:2 (id: 10019109002)

2For a wicked mouth and a mouth of deceit open against me; they speak a tongue of untruth with me,


## Psalms 109:3 (id: 10019109003)

3and words of hatred surround me, and they attack me without cause.


## Psalms 109:4 (id: 10019109004)

4In return for my love they accuse me, but I—prayer.


## Psalms 109:5 (id: 10019109005)

5They put upon me evil in return for good, and hatred in return for my love.


## Psalms 109:6 (id: 10019109006)

6Appoint a wicked one over him; and may an accuser stand at his right hand.


## Psalms 109:7 (id: 10019109007)

7When he is judged, may he come out guilty; and may his prayer be as sin.


## Psalms 109:8 (id: 10019109008)

8May his days be few; may another take his position.


## Psalms 109:9 (id: 10019109009)

## Psalms 150:1 (id: 10019150001)

## Psalms 150:2 (id: 10019150002)
